# Checkout (`/checkout`)

Source: logged-in live site `https://ecommerce-route-ashen.vercel.app/checkout`, captured at 1920 / 1024 / 390. Test email redacted. Cart/checkout/wishlist have no `<main>`; the saved content root is the page wrapper. Raw markup: `checkout.html`.

## 1920px / default

Content root: `<div class="bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-50 to-white min-h-screen py-8">` — 1920x1275 at (0, 113).
